# Graph Report - hse  (2026-11-23)

## Corpus Check
- 1240 files · ~1,870,053 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 27130 nodes · 44822 edges · 2794 communities (2464 shown, 330 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 81% EXTRACTED · 19% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 8465 edges (avg confidence: 0.51)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `5493288f`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
